cwic_country_add_profit_exxon = {
	# Adds this country's operating profit contribution for exxon into the global monthly pool.
	# Requires: global.CWIC_CORP_BASE_PROFIT_UNIT, CWIC_oil_output_index, and local presence var corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons (0..5)
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons }

		# presence_factor = presence / 5 (v1 linear)
		set_temp_variable = { p = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons }
		divide_temp_variable = { p = 5 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = p min = 0 max = 1 }

		# profit_add = BASE_UNIT * sector_index * presence_factor
		set_temp_variable = { profit_add = global.CWIC_CORP_BASE_PROFIT_UNIT }
		multiply_temp_variable = { profit_add = CWIC_oil_output_index }
		multiply_temp_variable = { profit_add = p }

		add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_profit_month = profit_add }
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = corp_inmetco_presence_mining }

		# presence_factor = presence / 5 (v1 linear)
		set_temp_variable = { p = corp_inmetco_presence_mining }
		divide_temp_variable = { p = 5 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = p min = 0 max = 1 }

		# profit_add = BASE_UNIT * sector_index * presence_factor
		set_temp_variable = { profit_add = global.CWIC_CORP_BASE_PROFIT_UNIT }
		multiply_temp_variable = { profit_add = CWIC_mining_output_index }
		multiply_temp_variable = { profit_add = p }

		add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_inmetco_profit_month = profit_add }
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { profit_add = global.CWIC_CORP_BASE_PROFIT_UNIT }
		multiply_temp_variable = { profit_add = CWIC_manufacturing_index }
		multiply_temp_variable = { profit_add = p }

		add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_peugeot_profit_month = profit_add }
	}
}

cwic_finalize_corp_exxon = {
	# Finalizes prior-month operating profit into cash/cap and sets this month's spend budget.

	# gross = last month's accumulated operating profit
	set_temp_variable = { gross = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_profit_month }

	# repatriated = gross * sector repatriation rate
	set_temp_variable = { rep = gross }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = global.CWIC_sector_repatriation_hydrocarbons }

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_profit_last = gross }
}

cwic_finalize_corp_mobil = {
	# Finalizes prior-month operating profit into cash/cap and sets this month's spend budget.

	# gross = last month's accumulated operating profit
	set_temp_variable = { gross = global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_profit_month }

	# repatriated = gross * sector repatriation rate
	set_temp_variable = { rep = gross }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = global.CWIC_sector_repatriation_hydrocarbons }

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_profit_last = gross }
}

	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_chevron_profit_last = gross }
}

cwic_finalize_corp_eni = {
	# Finalizes prior-month operating profit into cash/cap and sets this month's spend budget.

	# gross = last month's accumulated operating profit
	set_temp_variable = { gross = global.CWIC_CORP_eni_profit_month }

	# repatriated = gross * sector repatriation rate
	set_temp_variable = { rep = gross }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = global.CWIC_sector_repatriation_hydrocarbons }

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_eni_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_eni_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_eni_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_eni_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_eni_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_eni_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_eni_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_eni_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_eni_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_eni_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_eni_profit_last = gross }
}

		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_pemex_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_pemex_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_pemex_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_pemex_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pemex_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pemex_profit_last = gross }
}

cwic_finalize_corp_rio_tinto = {
	# Finalizes prior-month operating profit into cash/cap and sets this month's spend budget.

	# gross = last month's accumulated operating profit
	set_temp_variable = { gross = global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_profit_month }

	# repatriated = gross * sector repatriation rate
	set_temp_variable = { rep = gross }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = global.CWIC_sector_repatriation_mining }

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_rio_tinto_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping

}

	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)

	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)

	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }

		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash }

	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_adm_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_adm_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_adm_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_adm_profit_last = gross }
}

cwic_finalize_corp_bunge = {
	# Finalizes prior-month operating profit into cash/cap and sets this month's spend budget.

	# gross = last month's accumulated operating profit
	set_temp_variable = { gross = global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_profit_month }

	# repatriated = gross * sector repatriation rate
	set_temp_variable = { rep = gross }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = global.CWIC_sector_repatriation_agri }

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_profit_last = gross }
}

	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_vw_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_vw_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_vw_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_vw_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_vw_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_vw_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_vw_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_vw_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_vw_profit_last = gross }
}

cwic_finalize_corp_fiat = {
	# Finalizes prior-month operating profit into cash/cap and sets this month's spend budget.

	# gross = last month's accumulated operating profit
	set_temp_variable = { gross = global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_profit_month }

	# repatriated = gross * sector repatriation rate
	set_temp_variable = { rep = gross }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = global.CWIC_sector_repatriation_manufacturing }

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_fiat_profit_last = gross }
}

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_dow_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_dow_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_dow_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_dow_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_dow_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_dow_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_dow_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_dow_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_dow_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_dow_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_dow_profit_last = gross }
}

		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_philips_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_philips_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_philips_profit_last = gross }
}

cwic_finalize_corp_olivetti = {
	# Finalizes prior-month operating profit into cash/cap and sets this month's spend budget.

	# gross = last month's accumulated operating profit
	set_temp_variable = { gross = global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_profit_month }

	# repatriated = gross * sector repatriation rate
	set_temp_variable = { rep = gross }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = global.CWIC_sector_repatriation_electronics }

	# optional macro haircut from overall corporate risk (keeps cash from exploding in high-risk worlds)
	# hair = clamp(0.50..1.00, 1 - (CWIC_corporate_risk_index/200))
	set_temp_variable = { rf = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { rf = 200 }
	set_temp_variable = { hair = 1 }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { hair = rf }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = hair min = 0.5 max = 1.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { rep = hair }

	# cash += rep (cash persists)
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_cash = rep }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }

	# cap += rep * 0.25 (retained earnings expand long-run capacity; tune later)
	set_temp_variable = { cap_add = rep }
	multiply_temp_variable = { cap_add = 0.25 }
	add_to_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_cap = cap_add }

	# shock factor (per corp)
	set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 1.0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_shock_timer > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { shock_factor = 0.5 }
		# tick shock timer down (1 month)
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_shock_timer = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_shock_timer min = 0 max = 120 }
	}

	# desired_budget = cash * invest_rate * macro_risk_factor * shock_factor
	set_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_cash }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = global.CWIC_CORP_INVEST_RATE }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = CWIC_global_budget_risk_factor }
	multiply_temp_variable = { desired = shock_factor }

	# clamp desired budget
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = desired min = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MIN max = global.CWIC_CORP_BUDGET_MAX }

	# budget cannot exceed available cash
	set_temp_variable = { budget = desired }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { budget > global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_cash } }
		set_temp_variable = { budget = global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_cash }
	}

	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_budget = budget }

	# book-keeping
	set_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_olivetti_profit_last = gross }
}

cwic_presence_tier_multiplier = {
	# expects: temp var target_tier (1..5)
	# outputs: temp var tier_mult

	set_temp_variable = { tier_mult = 1.0 } # fallback

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { target_tier = 1 } } set_temp_variable = { tier_mult = 1.00 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { target_tier = 2 } } set_temp_variable = { tier_mult = 1.75 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { target_tier = 3 } } set_temp_variable = { tier_mult = 2.75 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { target_tier = 4 } } set_temp_variable = { tier_mult = 4.00 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { target_tier = 5 } } set_temp_variable = { tier_mult = 5.50 } }
	else = { set_temp_variable = { tier_mult = 6.50 } }
}
cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_hydrocarbons = {
	# expects: temp var target_tier (1..5)
	# outputs: temp var invest_cost (billions)

	cwic_presence_tier_multiplier = yes

	set_temp_variable = { invest_cost = global.CWIC_CORP_PRESENCE_UPGRADE_BASE_COST }
	multiply_temp_variable = { invest_cost = tier_mult }

	# host opportunity: oil market depth
	set_temp_variable = { tmp = CWIC_oil_output_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { tmp = 4 }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = tmp min = 0.5 max = 2.0 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { invest_cost = tmp }

	# risk premium
	set_temp_variable = { tmp = CWIC_corporate_risk_index }
	divide_temp_variable = { tmp = 200 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { tmp = 1 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { invest_cost = tmp }
}

# ============================================================
# CWIC MNC: Invest Presence Effects (sectorized) for all corps
# - Uses only >, <, = (no >= / <=).
# ============================================================

cwic_corp_exxon_invest_presence_hydrocarbons = {

	# Do nothing if no cash
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash > 0 } } }
	}
	else = {

		# Determine current tier
		set_temp_variable = { cur = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons }
			set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons }
		}

		# If already maxed (tier 5), stop
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { cur > 4 } }
		}
		else = {

			# target_tier = cur + 1 (or 1 if new)
			set_temp_variable = { target_tier = cur }
			add_to_temp_variable = { target_tier = 1 }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = target_tier min = 1 max = 5 }

			# compute invest_cost for this sector in this country
			cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_hydrocarbons = yes

			# Gate by cash and by budget remaining
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash = invest_cost }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons } }
					set_variable = { corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
					clamp_variable = { var = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons min = 0 max = 5 }
				}

				# Optional: host-country effects as presence rises (stub)
				# cwic_apply_corp_presence_effects_exxon = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

cwic_corp_shell_invest_presence_hydrocarbons = {

	# Do nothing if no cash
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_shell_cash > 0 } } }
	}
	else = {

		# Determine current tier
		set_temp_variable = { cur = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = corp_shell_presence_hydrocarbons }
			set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_shell_presence_hydrocarbons }
		}

		# If already maxed (tier 5), stop
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { cur > 4 } }
		}
		else = {

			# target_tier = cur + 1 (or 1 if new)
			set_temp_variable = { target_tier = cur }
			add_to_temp_variable = { target_tier = 1 }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = target_tier min = 1 max = 5 }

			# compute invest_cost for this sector in this country
			cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_hydrocarbons = yes

			# Gate by cash and by budget remaining
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_shell_cash > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_shell_cash = invest_cost }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_shell_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_shell_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_shell_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_shell_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_shell_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_shell_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_shell_presence_hydrocarbons } }
					set_variable = { corp_shell_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_shell_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
					clamp_variable = { var = corp_shell_presence_hydrocarbons min = 0 max = 5 }
				}

				# Optional: host-country effects as presence rises (stub)
				# cwic_apply_corp_presence_effects_shell = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_sonatrach_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_sonatrach_presence_hydrocarbons } }
					set_variable = { corp_sonatrach_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_sonatrach_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
					clamp_variable = { var = corp_sonatrach_presence_hydrocarbons min = 0 max = 5 }
				}

				# Optional: host-country effects as presence rises (stub)
				# cwic_apply_corp_presence_effects_sonatrach = yes

cwic_corp_pertamina_invest_presence_hydrocarbons = {

	# Do nothing if no cash
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_cash > 0 } } }
	}
	else = {

		# Determine current tier
		set_temp_variable = { cur = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = corp_pertamina_presence_hydrocarbons }
			set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_pertamina_presence_hydrocarbons }
		}

		# If already maxed (tier 5), stop
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { cur > 4 } }
		}
		else = {

			# target_tier = cur + 1 (or 1 if new)
			set_temp_variable = { target_tier = cur }
			add_to_temp_variable = { target_tier = 1 }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = target_tier min = 1 max = 5 }

			# compute invest_cost for this sector in this country
			cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_hydrocarbons = yes

			# Gate by cash and by budget remaining
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_cash > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_cash = invest_cost }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_pertamina_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_pertamina_presence_hydrocarbons } }
					set_variable = { corp_pertamina_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_pertamina_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 }
					clamp_variable = { var = corp_pertamina_presence_hydrocarbons min = 0 max = 5 }
				}

				# Optional: host-country effects as presence rises (stub)
				# cwic_apply_corp_presence_effects_pertamina = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_kennecott_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_kennecott_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_kennecott_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_kennecott_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_kennecott_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_kennecott_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_kennecott_presence_mining } }
					set_variable = { corp_kennecott_presence_mining = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_kennecott_presence_mining = 1 }
					clamp_variable = { var = corp_kennecott_presence_mining min = 0 max = 5 }
				}

				# Optional: host-country effects as presence rises (stub)
				# cwic_apply_corp_presence_effects_kennecott = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

cwic_corp_anaconda_copper_invest_presence_mining = {

	# Do nothing if no cash
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash > 0 } } }
	}
	else = {

		# Determine current tier
		set_temp_variable = { cur = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = corp_anaconda_copper_presence_mining }
			set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_anaconda_copper_presence_mining }
		}

		# If already maxed (tier 5), stop
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { cur > 4 } }
		}
		else = {

			# target_tier = cur + 1 (or 1 if new)
			set_temp_variable = { target_tier = cur }
			add_to_temp_variable = { target_tier = 1 }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = target_tier min = 1 max = 5 }

			# compute invest_cost for this sector in this country
			cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_mining = yes

			# Gate by cash and by budget remaining
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash = invest_cost }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_anaconda_copper_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {

			clamp_temp_variable = { var = target_tier min = 1 max = 5 }

			# compute invest_cost for this sector in this country
			cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_agri = yes

			# Gate by cash and by budget remaining
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash = invest_cost }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_bunge_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade

					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_bunge_presence_agri } }
					set_variable = { corp_bunge_presence_agri = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_bunge_presence_agri = 1 }

		# If already maxed (tier 5), stop
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { cur > 4 } }
		}
		else = {

			# target_tier = cur + 1 (or 1 if new)
			set_temp_variable = { target_tier = cur }
			add_to_temp_variable = { target_tier = 1 }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = target_tier min = 1 max = 5 }

			# compute invest_cost for this sector in this country
			cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_manufacturing = yes

			# Gate by cash and by budget remaining
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_renault_cash > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_renault_cash = invest_cost }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_renault_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_renault_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_renault_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_renault_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_renault_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_renault_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_renault_presence_manufacturing } }
					set_variable = { corp_renault_presence_manufacturing = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_renault_presence_manufacturing = 1 }
					clamp_variable = { var = corp_renault_presence_manufacturing min = 0 max = 5 }
				}

				# Optional: host-country effects as presence rises (stub)
				# cwic_apply_corp_presence_effects_renault = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = corp_ibm_presence_electronics }
			set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_ibm_presence_electronics }
		}

		# If already maxed (tier 5), stop
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { cur > 4 } }
		}
		else = {

			# target_tier = cur + 1 (or 1 if new)
			set_temp_variable = { target_tier = cur }
			add_to_temp_variable = { target_tier = 1 }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = target_tier min = 1 max = 5 }

			# compute invest_cost for this sector in this country
			cwic_calc_presence_upgrade_cost_electronics = yes

			# Gate by cash and by budget remaining
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_cash > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_cash = invest_cost }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_budget_remaining > invest_cost }
						check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
					}
				}

				# Spend
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_cash = invest_cost }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_budget_remaining = invest_cost }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_cash min = 0 max = 1000000 }
				clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_ibm_budget_remaining min = 0 max = 1000000 }

				# Apply: entry or upgrade
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_ibm_presence_electronics } }
					set_variable = { corp_ibm_presence_electronics = 1 }
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = { corp_ibm_presence_electronics = 1 }
					clamp_variable = { var = corp_ibm_presence_electronics min = 0 max = 5 }
				}

				# Optional: host-country effects as presence rises (stub)
				# cwic_apply_corp_presence_effects_ibm = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

cwic_mnc_process_invites_global = {
	# Scans ONLY countries flagged as having pending MNC invites.
	# Fires at most one invite event per eligible country per month (per-country cooldown).
	# Call this from a GLOBAL monthly pulse (after budgets are set).

			has_country_flag = cwic_has_pending_mnc_invite

# ============================================================
# CWIC MNC: Auto-Invest AI (v2 with Auto-Entry)
# File suggestion: common/scripted_effects/cwic_mnc_auto_invest.txt
# Notes:
# - Each month, each corp attempts at most ONE action.
# - Priority: upgrade existing presence; if none found, may attempt a new entry.
# - Auto-entry uses random chance and stricter market threshold.
# - Uses only > < = comparisons (no >= / <=).
# ============================================================

cwic_mnc_auto_invest_global = {
	# Call from GLOBAL monthly pulse after corp budgets are set,
	# and after countries have set pool flags in their country-monthly.
	# Auto-entry chance is tuned per-corp here. Change the chance value if needed.

	# --- exxon (hydrocarbons) ---
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash > 0 }
			check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_budget_remaining > 0 }
		}

		# 1) Try to upgrade existing presence
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = cwic_mnc_pool_hydrocarbons
				has_variable = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons
				OR = {
					cwic_mnc_country_open_for_investment = yes
					cwic_mnc_country_allows_foreign_concessions = yes
				}
			}
			cwic_corp_exxon_invest_presence_hydrocarbons = yes
		}

		# 2) If no existing host matched, try a new entry (low chance, stricter threshold)
		random = {
			chance = 15
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = cwic_mnc_pool_hydrocarbons
					NOT = { has_variable = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons }
					OR = {
						cwic_mnc_country_open_for_investment = yes
						cwic_mnc_country_allows_foreign_concessions = yes
					}
					check_variable = { CWIC_oil_output_index > 1.25 }
				}
				cwic_corp_exxon_invest_presence_hydrocarbons = yes
			}
		}
	}

	# --- mobil (hydrocarbons) ---
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_mobil_cash > 0 }

# ============================================================
# CWIC MNC: State Backlash (v1)
# Goal: Increase resistance/separatism only in low-HDI states when
#       extractive sector presence is high (hydrocarbons + mining).
#
# Place into: common/scripted_effects/cwic_mnc_state_backlash_effects.txt
# Call from COUNTRY monthly AFTER:
#  - cwic_mnc_apply_sector_presence_effects_country (so sector tiers exist)
#  - any separatism/resistance rollups you want as baseline
#
# Requires (existing in your mod):
#  - State_HDI (state variable)
#  - separatism (state variable used by calculate_separatism)
#  - CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_hydrocarbons, CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_mining (country vars)
# Notes:
#  - Uses only > < = comparisons (no >= / <=).
#  - Tuning knobs are at the top of the country effect.
# ============================================================

cwic_mnc_apply_state_backlash_effects_country = {
	# -------------------------
	# TUNING
	# -------------------------
	set_temp_variable = { HDI_LOW = 0.40 }              # only states below this HDI get backlash
	set_temp_variable = { RES_ADD_PER_POINT = 0.25 }   # add_resistance per extractive point
	set_temp_variable = { SEP_ADD_PER_POINT = 0.20 }   # separatism variable bump per extractive point (0..100 scale)
	set_temp_variable = { MAX_RES_ADD = 2.0 }          # clamp per-state monthly resistance add
	set_temp_variable = { MAX_SEP_ADD = 3.0 }          # clamp per-state monthly separatism add

	# -------------------------
	# Build extractive_pressure (0..6)
	# -------------------------
	set_temp_variable = { extractive_pressure = 0 }

	# Hydrocarbons tier contribution
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_hydrocarbons > 2 } } add_to_temp_variable = { extractive_pressure = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_hydrocarbons > 3 } } add_to_temp_variable = { extractive_pressure = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_hydrocarbons > 4 } } add_to_temp_variable = { extractive_pressure = 1 } }
	# Mining tier contribution
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_mining > 2 } } add_to_temp_variable = { extractive_pressure = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_mining > 3 } } add_to_temp_variable = { extractive_pressure = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { CWIC_mnc_presence_tier_mining > 4 } } add_to_temp_variable = { extractive_pressure = 1 } }

	# Early-out if no pressure
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { extractive_pressure = 0 } }
	}
	else = {
		# Apply backlash to low-HDI states
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { State_HDI < HDI_LOW }
			}

			# Compute adds
			set_temp_variable = { res_add = extractive_pressure }
			multiply_temp_variable = { res_add = RES_ADD_PER_POINT }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = res_add min = 0 max = MAX_RES_ADD }

			set_temp_variable = { sep_add = extractive_pressure }
			multiply_temp_variable = { sep_add = SEP_ADD_PER_POINT }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = sep_add min = 0 max = MAX_SEP_ADD }

			# Apply resistance bump
			add_resistance = res_add

			# Apply separatism bump
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = separatism } } set_variable = { separatism = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { separatism = sep_add }
			clamp_variable = { var = separatism min = 0 max = 100 }
		}
	}
}
cwic_mnc_nationalize_exxon_hydrocarbons = {
	# Targeted nationalization: Exxon (hydrocarbons)
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_exxon_hydrocarbons } }
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons }
		set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = cur }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_SEIZE_UNIT_HYDRO }
		set_temp_variable = { tmp = CWIC_oil_output_index }
		divide_temp_variable = { tmp = 4 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = tmp min = 0.5 max = 2.0 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = tmp }
		set_temp_variable = { corp_cash = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { seize_amount > corp_cash } } set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = corp_cash } }
		add_to_variable = { currentMoney = seize_amount }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash = seize_amount }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_exxon_cash min = 0 max = 100000000 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { cur = 1 } } clear_variable = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons }
		else = { subtract_from_variable = { corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons = 1 } clamp_variable = { var = corp_exxon_presence_hydrocarbons min = 0 max = 5 } }
		set_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_exxon_hydrocarbons
		set_country_flag = { flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_exxon_hydrocarbons days = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_FREEZE_DAYS }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index = 0.15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index min = 0 max = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_instability_index = 0.05 }

cwic_mnc_nationalize_inmetco_mining = {
	# Targeted nationalization: Inmetco (mining)
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_inmetco_presence_mining } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_inmetco_mining } }
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_inmetco_presence_mining }
		set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = cur }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_SEIZE_UNIT_MINING }
		set_temp_variable = { tmp = CWIC_mining_output_index }
		divide_temp_variable = { tmp = 3 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = tmp min = 0.5 max = 2.0 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = tmp }
		set_temp_variable = { corp_cash = global.CWIC_CORP_inmetco_cash }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { seize_amount > corp_cash } } set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = corp_cash } }
		add_to_variable = { currentMoney = seize_amount }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_inmetco_cash = seize_amount }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_inmetco_cash min = 0 max = 100000000 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { cur = 1 } } clear_variable = corp_inmetco_presence_mining }
		else = { subtract_from_variable = { corp_inmetco_presence_mining = 1 } clamp_variable = { var = corp_inmetco_presence_mining min = 0 max = 5 } }
		set_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_inmetco_mining
		set_country_flag = { flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_inmetco_mining days = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_FREEZE_DAYS }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index = 0.15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index min = 0 max = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_instability_index = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = { var = CWIC_instability_index min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
}

	# Targeted nationalization: ADM (agri)
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_adm_presence_agri } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_adm_agri } }
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_adm_presence_agri }
		set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = cur }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_SEIZE_UNIT_AGRI }
		set_temp_variable = { tmp = CWIC_agriculture_output_index }
		divide_temp_variable = { tmp = 3 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = tmp min = 0.5 max = 2.0 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = tmp }
		set_temp_variable = { corp_cash = global.CWIC_CORP_adm_cash }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { seize_amount > corp_cash } } set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = corp_cash } }
		add_to_variable = { currentMoney = seize_amount }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_adm_cash = seize_amount }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_adm_cash min = 0 max = 100000000 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { cur = 1 } } clear_variable = corp_adm_presence_agri }
		else = { subtract_from_variable = { corp_adm_presence_agri = 1 } clamp_variable = { var = corp_adm_presence_agri min = 0 max = 5 } }
		set_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_adm_agri
		set_country_flag = { flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_adm_agri days = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_FREEZE_DAYS }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index = 0.15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index min = 0 max = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_instability_index = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = { var = CWIC_instability_index min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
}

cwic_mnc_nationalize_peugeot_manufacturing = {
	# Targeted nationalization: Peugeot (manufacturing)
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = corp_peugeot_presence_manufacturing } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_peugeot_manufacturing } }
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { cur = corp_peugeot_presence_manufacturing }
		set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = cur }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_SEIZE_UNIT_MFG }
		set_temp_variable = { tmp = CWIC_manufacturing_index }
		divide_temp_variable = { tmp = 4 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = tmp min = 0.5 max = 2.0 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { seize_amount = tmp }
		set_temp_variable = { corp_cash = global.CWIC_CORP_peugeot_cash }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { seize_amount > corp_cash } } set_temp_variable = { seize_amount = corp_cash } }
		add_to_variable = { currentMoney = seize_amount }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.CWIC_CORP_peugeot_cash = seize_amount }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.CWIC_CORP_peugeot_cash min = 0 max = 100000000 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { cur = 1 } } clear_variable = corp_peugeot_presence_manufacturing }
		else = { subtract_from_variable = { corp_peugeot_presence_manufacturing = 1 } clamp_variable = { var = corp_peugeot_presence_manufacturing min = 0 max = 5 } }
		set_country_flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_peugeot_manufacturing
		set_country_flag = { flag = cwic_mnc_frozen_peugeot_manufacturing days = global.CWIC_MNC_NAT_FREEZE_DAYS }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index = 0.15 }
		clamp_variable = { var = CWIC_nationalization_pressure_index min = 0 max = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { CWIC_instability_index = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = { var = CWIC_instability_index min = 0 max = 3 }
	}
}
